Combination of TCM and western medicine.                                    中醫與西醫相結合 。

We originated from the conventional TCM diagnosis and treatment             我們從傳統的中醫基礎醫療診療法出發 ，
methods of primary care and developed diagnosis and treatment               發展出中醫與西醫相結合的診療法 。 我們
methods combining TCM and western medicine. We provide TCM                  通過線下和線上相結合的醫療服務網絡提
healthcare solutions through our integrated offline and online medical      供中醫醫療健康解決方案 ， 將傳統中醫診
service network, combining conventional TCM diagnosis and treatment         療 方 法 與 西 醫（例 如 臨 床 檢 查 和 治 療）相 結
methods with western medicine, such as clinical laboratory examination      合 。 我們旨在有效 、 高效地為客戶提供全
and treatment. We aim to effectively and efficiently provide customers      面的醫療健康解決方案 ， 尤其是慢性病管
with comprehensive healthcare solutions, especially chronic disease         理 ， 從而滿足客戶多樣化的醫療健康管理
management, to address their diverse medical and health management          需求 。 我們以客戶日常的基礎護理為重點 ，
needs. We focus on the customers’ daily primary care, aiming to achieve     旨在實現對客戶的長期隨訪及健康管理 。
long-term follow-up and health management for customers.


Standardized and digitalized operations.                                    標準化及數字化營運 。

digitalization of our operations to provide an optimized customer           而提供優化的客戶體驗並在醫療服務網絡
experience and achieve better operational efficiency as well as resource    內實現更高的營運效率以及更好的資源分配 。
sharing within our medical service network.

     to improve customer experience. In the meantime, the digital                同時數字化店員系統促進了我們與客

     feedback collection from customers, thereby empowering our                  提升線下醫療機構的客戶就診人次及
     overall business through increasing customer visits and customer            客戶回頭率 ， 及在線下醫療機構實施
     return rates of and implementing digital management in our offline          數字化管理 ， 為整體業務賦能 。
     medical institutions.

     system on the medical-affair side to integrate our development              （「CRM」）系 統 ， 以 整 合 對 醫 療 專 業
     and management of medical professional teams. Capitalizing on               團 隊 的 開 發 和 管 理 。 利 用 CRM 系 統 ，
     the CRM system, we are able to conduct digital analytics on daily           我們能夠使用數字化統計對醫療專業

In March 2022, the Physician Law of the People’s Republic of China             於2022年3月 ，《中 華 人 民 共 和 國 醫 師 法》生
(《中 華 人 民 共 和 國 醫 師 法》) came into effect. This law encourages                  效 。 該法鼓勵醫師定期定點到縣級以下醫
physicians to regularly provide healthcare services at medical institutions    療衛生機構 ， 提供醫療衛生服務 ， 醫師的
at or below the county level, which should be supported by their primary       主執業機構應當支持 。 在同一個月 ， 國家

Non-HKFRS Measure — Adjusted Net Profit                                     非香港財務報告準則計量 — 經調整
                                                                            溢利淨額

To supplement our financial information, which is presented in              為補充按照香港財務報告準則呈列的財務
accordance with HKFRS, we also provide adjusted net profit as non-          資料 ， 我們亦提供經調整溢利淨額作為非
HKFRS measure, which is unaudited in nature and is not required             香港財務報告準則計量 ， 其屬未經審核性
by, or presented in accordance with, HKFRS. We believe that the             質 ， 並非香港財務報告準則規定或根據香
non-HKFRS measure (i) facilitates period-to-period comparisons of           港財務報告準則呈列 。 我們認為 ， 非香港
operating performance by eliminating potential impacts of items that        財 務 報 告 準 則 計 量 (i) 消 除 管 理 層 認 為 並 非 營
our management does not consider to be indicative of our operating          運表現指標項目的潛在影響 ， 有助比較同
performance; and (ii) provides useful information to investors in           比 營 運 表 現 ； 及 (ii) 如 同 協 助 管 理 層 般 ， 為
understanding and evaluating our results of consolidated statements of      投資者提供有用資料 ， 以了解及評估綜合
profit or loss and other comprehensive income in the same manner as         損益及其他全面收益表的業績 。
they helped our management.


However, our presentation of adjusted net profit may not be comparable      然而 ， 我們呈列的經調整溢利淨額不可與
to similarly titled measures presented by other companies as they do        其他公司所呈列類似名稱的計量作比較 ，
not have a standardized meaning. As an analytical tool, the application     因為其並無標準意義 。 作為分析工具 ， 應
of the non-HKFRS measure has limitations, and the Shareholders and          用非香港財務報告準則計量有其限制 ， 故
investors should not consider the non-HKFRS measure in isolation from,      股東及投資者不應對非香港財務報告準則計
or as substitute for analysis of, our results of operations or financial    量單獨考慮 ， 或以其代替我們根據香港財
condition as reported under HKFRS.                                          務報告準則呈報的經營業績或財務狀況分析 。

We defined adjusted net profit as profit for the period adjusted for        我們將經調整溢利淨額界定為已就與下列
equity-settled share-based payments in relation to (i) share options        項目有關的以權益結算以股份為基礎的付
granted under the Pre-IPO Share Option Plan, (ii) share options granted     款 作 出 調 整 的 期 內 溢 利 ： (i) 根 據 首 次 公 開 發
under the Post-IPO Share Option Scheme, (iii) share awards granted          售 前 購 股 權 計 劃 授 出 的 購 股 權 ； (ii) 根 據 首 次
under the RSA Scheme (Existing Shares), and (iv) share awards granted       公 開 發 售 後 購 股 權 計 劃 授 出 的 購 股 權 ； (iii)
under the RSA Scheme (New Shares). We eliminate the potential               根 據 受 限 制 股 份 獎 勵 計 劃（現 有 股 份）授 出
impacts of such item that our management does not consider to be            的 股 份 獎 勵 ； 及 (iv) 根 據 受 限 制 股 份 獎 勵 計
indicative of our operating performance, as such equity-settled share-      劃（新 股 份）授 出 的 股 份 獎 勵 。 我 們 剔 除 管
based payments are non-operating and non-recurring expenses of our          理層認為並非營運表現指標項目的潛在影
Group. Adjusted net profit increased by 45.3% from RMB101.8 million         響 ， 原因是該等以權益結算以股份為基礎
for the six months ended June 30, 2023 to RMB147.9 million for the six      的付款為本集團非經營性及非經常性開支 。
months ended June 30, 2024.                                                 經 調 整 溢 利 淨 額 由 截 至2023年6月30日 止 六

We aim to maintain sufficient cash and credit lines to meet our liquidity         我們旨在維持充足的現金及信貸額度以滿
requirements. We finance our working capital requirements through a               足流動資金需求 。 我們透過營運所得資金
combination of funds generated from operations and alternative funding            以及來自權益及債務的替代性資金來源滿
resources from equity and debt. The primary objectives of our capital             足我們的營運資金需求 。 我們資本管理的
management are to safeguard our ability to continue as a going concern            主要目標是保障持續經營的能力 ， 同時維
and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support our business           持健康的資本比率 ， 以支持業務並使股東
and maximize Shareholders’ value.                                                 價值最大化 。

controlled at the corporate level. The Board closely monitors our liquidity       理和控制 。 董事會密切監控我們的流動資
position to ensure that the liquidity structure of our assets, liabilities and    金狀況 ， 確保資產 、 負債及其他承諾的流
other commitments can meet our funding requirements all the time.                 動性結構始終能滿足資金需求 。

Foreign Currency Risk                                                             外匯風險

We have transactional currency exposures. Such exposures arise from               我們有交易貨幣風險 。 有關風險來自於使
the use of financial instruments denominated in USD or HKD to finance             用以美元或港元計值的金融工具為我們於
our operations in the PRC and the fact that the repayment of those                中國的業務提供資金 ， 但該等以美元計值
USD-denominated or HKD-denominated financial instruments is based                 或以港元計值的金融工具乃根據我們於中
on the RMB-denominated assets generated by our PRC operations. We                 國的業務所產生以人民幣計值的資產進行
have no foreign currency hedging policy. However, our management                  還款 。 我們並無外匯對沖政策 。 然而 ， 管
monitors foreign exchange exposures and will consider appropriate                 理層會監控外匯風險 ， 並將在未來有需要
hedging measures in the future should the need arise.                             時考慮採取適當的對沖措施 。

COMPLIANCE WITH THE CORPORATE                                              遵守企業管治守則
GOVERNANCE CODE

The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate          本集團致力維持高水平的企業管治 ， 以維
governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to           護股東的權益並改善企業價值及問責性 。
enhance corporate value and accountability. The Company has adopted        本 公 司 已 採 納 上 市 規 則 附 錄 C1所 載 企 業 管
the CG Code as set out in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules as its own      治守則作為其自身的企業管治守則 。
code of corporate governance.


The Board considered that the Company has complied with all                董 事 會 認 為 ， 本 公 司 於 截 至2024年6月30日
applicable code provisions as set out in part 2 of the CG Code during      止六個月已遵守企業管治守則第二部分所
the six months ended June 30, 2024, save and except for code               載的所有適用守則條文 ， 惟企業管治守則
provision C.2.1 in part 2 of the CG Code.                                  第 二 部 分 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 除 外 。


Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 in part 2 of the CG Code, the roles       根 據 企 業 管 治 守 則 第 二 部 分 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1
of chairman and chief executive officer should be separate and should      條 ， 主席及首席執行官的角色應有所區分 ，
not be performed by the same individual. Mr. Tu is currently serving       不應由一人同時兼任 。 涂先生現任本公司
as the chairman of the Board as well as the chief executive officer        董事會主席兼首席執行官 。 由於涂先生為
of our Company. As Mr. Tu is the founder of our Group and has              本集團的創始人 ， 自本集團成立起一直管
been managing our Group’s business and overall strategic planning          理本集團的業務及整體戰略規劃 ， 故董事
since its establishment, the Directors consider that vesting the roles     認為 ， 將主席及首席執行官職位授予涂先
of chairman and chief executive officer in Mr. Tu is beneficial to the     生 ， 有利於本集團的業務前景及管理 ， 確
business prospects and management of our Group by ensuring                 保本集團內的一貫領導 。 因此 ， 董事會認
consistent leadership within our Group. The Board therefore considers      為 ， 在有關情況下偏離企業管治守則第二
it is appropriate to deviate from code provision C.2.1 in part 2 of        部 分 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 屬 恰 當 之 舉 。 如 有
the CG Code in such circumstances. The Board will continue to              需要 ， 經考慮本集團的整體情況 ， 董事會
review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of our Board and       將繼續檢討並於適當時候考慮分拆本公司
the chief executive officer of the Company at an appropriate time if       董事會主席及首席執行官的角色 。 儘管有
necessary, taking into account the circumstances of our Group as a         上述規定 ， 董事會認為管理架構對本集團
whole. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the Board considers that the         營運有效 ， 且已經建立足夠的制衡機制 。
management structure is effective for the operation of the Group and
those adequate checks and balances have been put in place.

  1   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                     1   編製基準

      The interim condensed consolidated financial information for                 截 至2024年6月30日 止 六 個 月 的 中 期 簡
      the six months ended June 30, 2024 has been prepared in                      明綜合財務資料乃根據香港會計準則
      accordance with HKAS 34 Interim Financial Reporting . The interim            第34號 中 期 財 務 報 告 編 製 。 中 期 簡 明
      condensed consolidated financial information does not include all            綜合財務資料並未包括年度財務報表
      the information and disclosures required in the annual financial             所需的所有資料及披露 ， 並須與本集
      statements, and should be read in conjunction with the Group’s               團 截 至2023年12月31日 止 年 度 的 年 度
      annual consolidated financial statements for the year ended                  綜合財務報表一併閱讀 。
      December 31, 2023.


      The unaudited interim condensed consolidated financial                       未經審核中期簡明綜合財務資料乃以
      information is presented in RMB and all value are rounded to the             人民幣呈列 ， 除另有說明者外 ， 所有
      nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.                            數值均四捨五入至最接近的千位 。

      for the first time for the current period’s financial information.           準則除外 。

3   OPERATING SEGMENT INFORMATION                                          3   經營分部資料

    The Group is principally engaged in the provision of a variety             本集團主要從事提供各種醫療健康業
    of healthcare businesses, including the provision of healthcare            務 ， 包括提供醫療健康服務和銷售醫
    services and sale of healthcare products.                                  療健康產品 。


    HKFRS 8 Operating Segments requires operating segments to                  香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第8號 經 營 分 部 規

    of the Group that are regularly reviewed by the chief operating            配資源予各分部及評估其表現而定期
    decision-maker in order to allocate resources to segments and              審閱的有關本集團組成部分的內部報
    to assess their performance. The information reported to the               告為基礎而區分 。 為進行資源分配及
    directors of the Company, who are the chief operating decision-            表 現 評 估 而 向 本 公 司 董 事（主 要 經 營
    makers, for the purpose of resource allocation and assessment of           決 策 者）報 告 的 資 料 並 不 包 含 不 連 續
    performance does not contain discrete operating segment financial          的經營分部財務資料 ， 且董事審閱本

8   INCOME TAX                                                             8   所得稅

    Pursuant to the PRC Income Tax Law and the respective                      根據中國所得稅法及相關規定 ， 於中
    regulations, a subsidiary which operates in Chinese Mainland is            國內地經營的附屬公司須按應課稅收
    subject to corporate income tax at a rate of 25% on the taxable            入 的25% 繳 納 企 業 所 得 稅 。 於 相 關 期
    income. Certain subsidiaries of the Group in Chinese Mainland              間 ， 本集團的部分中國內地附屬公司
    are regarded as “high and new technology enterprise” and a                 被 視 為「高 新 技 術 企 業」， 享 有15% 的
    preferential income tax rate of 15% during the Relevant Periods.           優惠所得稅稅率 。 於報告期內 ， 本集
    Certain subsidiaries of the Group in Chinese Mainland are regarded         團 的 部 分 中 國 內 地 附 屬 公 司 被 視 為「小
    as “small and micro enterprises” and, accordingly, were entitled to        微 企 業」， 因 此 有 權 按5% 的 優 惠 所 得
    a preferential income tax rate of 5% during the reporting period.          稅稅率繳稅 。 其他地方應課稅溢利之
    Taxes on profits assessable elsewhere have been calculated at              稅項已按本集團經營所在司法權區之
    the rates of tax prevailing in the jurisdictions in which the Group        現行稅率計算 。
    operates.

   used to estimate the fair values:
